============================================================
SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
============================================================

This file is rebuilt from the current Portfolio workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.

The snapshot intentionally ignores generated/cache/runtime/private files:
- .git
- node_modules
- .next
- .next.*
- out
- dist
- build
- .cache
- .turbo
- .npm
- .pnpm-store
- .yarn
- .DS_Store
- next-env.d.ts
- *.tsbuildinfo
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.zip
- .env and .env*.local

Binary image, PDF, and archive files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.

     1	import type { Metadata, Viewport } from "next";

     7	  subsets: ["latin"],
     8	  style: "italic",
     9	  variable: "--font-eb-garamond",

   710	function PhonePreview({
   711	  title,
   712	  metric,
   713	  variant,
   714	  href,
   715	  screenImageSrc,
   716	  screenImageAlt,
   717	}: {
   718	  href?: string;
   719	  title: string;
   720	  metric: string;
   721	  variant: "commerce" | "finance";
   722	  screenImageSrc?: string;
   723	  screenImageAlt?: string;
   724	}) {
   725	  const className = `phone-shell phone-shell-${variant}${
   726	    screenImageSrc ? " phone-shell-capture" : ""
   727	  }${href ? " phone-shell-link" : ""}`;
   728	  const content = screenImageSrc ? (
   729	    <Image
   730	      alt={screenImageAlt ?? `${title} app screen`}
   731	      className="phone-framed-image"
   732	      fill
   733	      sizes="245px"
   734	      src={screenImageSrc}
   735	    />
   736	  ) : (
   737	    <>
   738	      <div className="phone-speaker" />
   739	      <div className="phone-screen">
   740	        <div className="phone-status">
   741	          <span>{title}</span>
   742	          <span>5G</span>
   743	        </div>
   744	        <div className="phone-chart">
   745	          <div />
   746	          <div />
   747	          <div />
   748	          <div />
   749	        </div>
   750	        <div className="phone-cardline wide" />
   751	        <div className="phone-cardline" />
   752	        <div className="phone-grid">
   753	          <span />
   754	          <span />
   755	          <span />
   756	          <span />
   757	        </div>
   758	        <div className="phone-cta">{metric}</div>
   759	      </div>
   760	    </>
   761	  );
   762	
   763	  if (href) {
   764	    return (
   765	      <a
   766	        aria-label={`Open ${title} case study`}
   767	        className={className}
   768	        href={href}
   769	      >
   770	        {content}
   771	      </a>
   772	    );
   773	  }
   774	
   775	  return <div className={className}>{content}</div>;
   776	}

    74	function getCaseStudyRowScrollOffset() {
    75	  const isWideLandscape = window.matchMedia(
    76	    "(min-width: 1024px) and (orientation: landscape)",
    77	  ).matches;
    78	  const isPortrait = window.matchMedia("(orientation: portrait)").matches;
    79	
    80	  if (isWideLandscape) {
    81	    return Math.max(18, Math.min(44, window.innerHeight * 0.035));
    82	  }
    83	
    84	  if (isPortrait) {
    85	    return Math.max(14, Math.min(36, window.innerHeight * 0.03));
    86	  }
    87	
    88	  return Math.max(16, Math.min(40, window.innerHeight * 0.0325));
    89	}
    90	
    91	function scrollCaseStudyRowIntoView(caseStudyId: string) {
    92	  const targetCard = document.getElementById(caseStudyId);
    93	
    94	  if (!targetCard) {
    95	    return;
    96	  }
    97	
    98	  const caseGrid = targetCard.closest<HTMLElement>(".case-grid");
    99	  const targetTop = targetCard.getBoundingClientRect().top;
   100	  const rowCards = caseGrid
   101	    ? Array.from(caseGrid.children).filter((child): child is HTMLElement => {
   102	        if (!(child instanceof HTMLElement)) {
   103	          return false;
   104	        }
   105	
   106	        const cardTop = child.getBoundingClientRect().top;
   107	
   108	        return Math.abs(cardTop - targetTop) <= 8;
   109	      })
   110	    : [targetCard];
   111	  const rowTop = Math.min(
   112	    ...rowCards.map((card) => card.getBoundingClientRect().top),
   113	  );
   114	
   115	  window.scrollTo({
   116	    behavior: "smooth",
   117	    top: window.scrollY + rowTop - getCaseStudyRowScrollOffset(),
   118	  });
   119	
   120	  window.history.replaceState(null, "", `#${caseStudyId}`);
   121	}

   142	    />
   143	  );

   171	function PhoneMark({
   172	  mobilePlatforms,
   173	}: {
   174	  mobilePlatforms: MobilePlatform[];
   175	}) {
   176	  return (
   177	    <svg
   178	      aria-hidden
   179	      className="carousel-button-phone"
   180	      height="1.936em"
   181	      style={{ height: "1.936em", width: "1.36125em" }}
   182	      viewBox="0 0 22.5 32"
   183	      width="1.36125em"
   184	    >
   185	      <rect
   186	        className="phone-mini-frame"
   187	        height="29.2"
   188	        rx="2.7"
   189	        width="19"
   190	        x="1.75"
   191	        y="1.4"
   192	      />
   193	      <rect
   194	        className="phone-mini-screen"
   195	        height="22.8"
   196	        rx="1.4"
   197	        width="14.5"
   198	        x="4"
   199	        y="4.6"
   200	      />
   201	      <path className="phone-mini-speaker" d="M8.875 3.2h4.75" />
   202	      <circle className="phone-mini-home" cx="11.25" cy="29" r="0.7" />
   203	      {mobilePlatforms.map((platform, index) => {
   204	        const slot =
   205	          mobilePlatforms.length === 1
   206	            ? "single"
   207	            : index === 0
   208	              ? "top"
   209	              : "bottom";
   210	
   211	        return platform === "android" ? (
   212	          <PhoneAndroidIcon
   213	            key={platform}
   214	            slot={slot === "bottom" ? "top" : slot}
   215	          />
   216	        ) : (
   217	          <PhoneAppleIcon
   218	            key={platform}
   219	            slot={slot === "top" ? "bottom" : slot}
   220	          />
   221	        );
   222	      })}
   223	    </svg>
   224	  );
   225	}

   239	      width="2.16em"
   240	    >
   241	      <rect
   242	        className="tablet-mini-frame"
   243	        fill="none"
   244	        height="21.2"
   245	        rx="3"
   246	        stroke="#000"
   247	        strokeWidth="2.2"
   248	        width="33.2"
   249	        x="1.4"
   250	        y="1.4"
   251	      />
   252	      <rect
   253	        className="tablet-mini-screen"
   254	        fill="transparent"
   255	        height="16.8"
   256	        rx="1.4"
   257	        stroke="none"
   258	        width="27.8"
   259	        x="4.8"
   260	        y="3.6"
   261	      />
   262	      <circle
   263	        className="tablet-mini-camera"
   264	        cx="3.2"
   265	        cy="12"
   266	        fill="#000"
   267	        r="0.65"
   268	        stroke="none"
   269	      />
   270	      {showPlatformIcon ? <AndroidLogoImage x={13.26608} y={7.65486} /> : null}
   271	    </svg>
   272	  );
   273	}
   274	
   275	function WebMark({ scale = 1 }: { scale?: number }) {
   276	  const size = `${(1.2 * scale).toFixed(3)}em`;
   277	
   278	  return (
   279	    <svg
   280	      aria-hidden
   281	      className="carousel-button-web"
   282	      height={size}
   283	      style={{ height: size, width: size }}
   284	      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
   285	      width={size}
   286	    >
   287	      <circle className="web-globe-shell" cx="12" cy="12" r="8.2" />
   288	      <path className="web-globe-line" d="M3.8 12h16.4" />
   289	      <path className="web-globe-line" d="M5.6 8.1h12.8" />
   290	      <path className="web-globe-line" d="M5.6 15.9h12.8" />
   291	      <path className="web-globe-line" d="M12 3.8c2.2 2.3 3.3 5 3.3 8.2s-1.1 5.9-3.3 8.2" />
   292	      <path className="web-globe-line" d="M12 3.8c-2.2 2.3-3.3 5-3.3 8.2s1.1 5.9 3.3 8.2" />
   293	    </svg>
   294	  );
   295	}
   296	
   297	const platformLabelColumnStart = 3;
   298	
   299	function getPlatformLabelGridColumn(index: number) {
   300	  return platformLabelColumnStart + index * 2;
   301	}
   302	
   303	function getPlatformDividerGridColumn(index: number) {
   304	  return getPlatformLabelGridColumn(index) + 1;
   305	}
   306	
   307	function getPlatformListGridTemplate(columnCount: number) {
   308	  const columns = ["92px", "minmax(8px, 1fr)"];
   309	
   310	  Array.from({ length: columnCount }).forEach((_, index) => {
   311	    columns.push("max-content");
   312	
   313	    if (index < columnCount - 1) {
   314	      columns.push("0px");
   315	    }
   316	  });
   317	
   318	  return columns.join(" ");
   319	}
   320	
   321	function PlatformIconSlot({
   322	  gridColumn,
   323	  gridRow,
   324	  label,
   325	  slotIndex,
   326	}: {
   327	  gridColumn?: number;
   328	  gridRow?: number;
   329	  label: string;
   330	  slotIndex?: number;
   331	}) {
   332	  return (
   333	    <span
   334	      className="case-card-title-platform-slot"
   335	      data-platform-index={slotIndex}
   336	      data-platform-label={label}
   337	      data-platform-row={gridRow}
   338	      style={
   339	        gridColumn && gridRow
   340	          ? {
   341	              gridColumn: String(gridColumn),
   342	              gridRow: String(gridRow),
   343	            }
   344	          : undefined
   345	      }
   346	    >
   347	    </span>
   348	  );
   349	}
   350	
   351	function PlatformLabelDivider({
   352	  className,
   353	  gridColumn,
   354	  gridRow,
   355	  kind,
   356	}: {
   357	  className?: string;
   358	  gridColumn?: number;
   359	  gridRow?: number | string;
   360	  kind: "grid" | "item";
   361	}) {
   362	  const dividerClassName = [
   363	    `case-card-title-platform-divider case-card-title-platform-${kind}-divider`,
   364	    className,
   365	  ]
   366	    .filter(Boolean)
   367	    .join(" ");
   368	
   369	  return (
   370	    <span
   371	      aria-hidden="true"
   372	      className={dividerClassName}
   373	      style={
   374	        gridColumn && gridRow
   375	          ? {
   376	              gridColumn: String(gridColumn),
   377	              gridRow: String(gridRow),
   378	            }
   379	          : undefined
   380	      }
   381	    />
   382	  );
   383	}
   384	
   385	function PlatformLabelSlots({
   386	  labels,
   387	  row,
   388	}: {
   389	  labels: string[];
   390	  row: number;
   391	}) {
   392	  return labels.map((label, index) => (
   393	    <PlatformIconSlot
   394	      gridColumn={getPlatformLabelGridColumn(index)}
   395	      gridRow={row}
   396	      key={`${row}-${label}`}
   397	      label={label}
   398	      slotIndex={index}
   399	    />
   400	  ));
   401	}
   402	
   403	function PlatformColumnDividers({
   404	  columnCount,
   405	  gridRow,
   406	  partial,
   407	  startIndex = 0,
   408	}: {
   409	  columnCount: number;
   410	  gridRow: number | string;
   411	  partial?: boolean;
   412	  startIndex?: number;
   413	}) {
   414	  return Array.from({ length: Math.max(0, columnCount - 1 - startIndex) }).map(
   415	    (_, index) => {
   416	      const dividerIndex = startIndex + index;
   417	
   418	      return (
   419	        <PlatformLabelDivider
   420	          className={
   421	            partial
   422	              ? "case-card-title-platform-partial-divider"
   423	              : undefined
   424	          }
   425	          gridColumn={getPlatformDividerGridColumn(dividerIndex)}
   426	          gridRow={gridRow}
   427	          key={`divider-${gridRow}-${dividerIndex}`}
   428	          kind="item"
   429	        />
   430	      );
   431	    },
   432	  );
   433	}
   434

   437	    <svg
   438	      aria-hidden
   439	      className="case-card-title-platform-mark case-card-title-platform-mark-android"
   440	      viewBox="4 3.4 16 12.8"
   441	    >
   442	      <path
   443	        d="M7.6 7.2 5.7 4.7"
   444	        fill="none"
   445	        stroke="#000"
   446	        strokeLinecap="round"
   447	        strokeWidth="2.4"
   448	      />
   449	      <path
   450	        d="m16.4 7.2 1.9-2.5"
   451	        fill="none"
   452	        stroke="#000"
   453	        strokeLinecap="round"
   454	        strokeWidth="2.4"
   455	      />
   456	      <path
   457	        d="M5.5 14a6.5 6.5 0 0 1 13 0v1H5.5z"
   458	        fill="#f3fff7"
   459	        stroke="#000"
   460	        strokeLinecap="round"
   461	        strokeLinejoin="round"
   462	        strokeWidth="2.4"
   463	      />
   464	      <circle cx="9.7" cy="11.7" fill="#000" r="0.56" />
   465	      <circle cx="14.3" cy="11.7" fill="#000" r="0.56" />
   466	    </svg>
   467	  );
   468	}

   497	function PlatformLogoMark({ platform }: { platform: MobilePlatform }) {
   498	  return platform === "android" ? (
   499	    <AndroidPlatformMark />
   500	  ) : (
   501	    <ApplePlatformMark />
   502	  );
   503	}
   504	
   505	function FactorDeviceMark({ device }: { device: "phone" | "tablet" }) {
   506	  return device === "phone" ? (
   507	    <PhoneMark mobilePlatforms={[]} />
   508	  ) : (
   509	    <TabletMark showPlatformIcon={false} />
   510	  );
   511	}
   512	
   513	export function ProjectDeviceStack({
   514	  assetGap,
   515	  color,
   516	  hasTablet,
   517	  hasWeb,
   518	  deviceLabels,
   519	  mobilePlatforms,
   520	  platformLabels: customPlatformLabels,
   521	  responsiveAssetGap,
   522	  showPlatformLabels,
   523	  webIconScale,
   524	}: {
   525	  assetGap?: string;
   526	  color: string;
   527	  deviceLabels?: string[];
   528	  hasTablet?: boolean;
   529	  hasWeb?: boolean;
   530	  mobilePlatforms: MobilePlatform[];
   531	  platformLabels?: string[];
   532	  responsiveAssetGap?: ResponsiveAssetGap;
   533	  showPlatformLabels?: boolean;
   534	  webIconScale?: number;
   535	}) {
   536	  const stackRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null);
   537	  const [measuredAssetGap, setMeasuredAssetGap] = useState<string | null>(null);
   538	  const mobilePlatformKey = mobilePlatforms.join(",");
   539	  const stackStyle: CSSProperties & {
   540	    "--project-device-stack-gap"?: string;
   541	  } = {
   542	    color,
   543	  };
   544	  const stackGap = measuredAssetGap ?? assetGap;
   545	  const deviceLabelKey = deviceLabels?.join(",") ?? "";
   546	  const platformLabelKey = customPlatformLabels?.join(",") ?? "";
   547	
   548	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   549	    if (!responsiveAssetGap) {
   550	      setMeasuredAssetGap(null);
   551	      return;
   552	    }
   553	
   554	    const stack = stackRef.current;
   555	    const measurementTarget = stack?.parentElement ?? stack;
   556	
   557	    if (!measurementTarget) {
   558	      return;
   559	    }
   560	
   561	    let frame = 0;
   562	    const updateGap = () => {
   563	      if (frame) {
   564	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   565	      }
   566	
   567	      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   568	        const laneWidth = measurementTarget.getBoundingClientRect().width;
   569	        const activeRange = Math.max(
   570	          1,
   571	          responsiveAssetGap.wideLanePx - responsiveAssetGap.tightLanePx,
   572	        );
   573	        const progress = Math.min(
   574	          1,
   575	          Math.max(
   576	            0,
   577	            (laneWidth - responsiveAssetGap.tightLanePx) / activeRange,
   578	          ),
   579	        );
   580	        const nextGap =
   581	          responsiveAssetGap.minPx +
   582	          (responsiveAssetGap.maxPx - responsiveAssetGap.minPx) * progress;
   583	        const nextGapValue = `${nextGap.toFixed(2)}px`;
   584	
   585	        setMeasuredAssetGap((currentGap) =>
   586	          currentGap === nextGapValue ? currentGap : nextGapValue,
   587	        );
   588	      });
   589	    };
   590	
   591	    updateGap();
   592	
   593	    const resizeObserver =
   594	      typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined"
   595	        ? null
   596	        : new ResizeObserver(updateGap);
   597	
   598	    resizeObserver?.observe(measurementTarget);
   599	    window.addEventListener("resize", updateGap);
   600	
   601	    return () => {
   602	      if (frame) {
   603	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   604	      }
   605	
   606	      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
   607	      window.removeEventListener("resize", updateGap);
   608	    };
   609	  }, [
   610	    responsiveAssetGap?.maxPx,
   611	    responsiveAssetGap?.minPx,
   612	    responsiveAssetGap?.tightLanePx,
   613	    responsiveAssetGap?.wideLanePx,
   614	  ]);
   615	
   616	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   617	    if (!showPlatformLabels) {
   618	      return;
   619	    }
   620	
   621	    const stack = stackRef.current;
   622	    const rowDivider = stack?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
   623	      ".case-card-title-platform-row-divider",
   624	    );
   625	
   626	    if (!stack || !rowDivider) {
   627	      return;
   628	    }
   629	
   630	    let frame = 0;
   631	    const updateRowDivider = () => {
   632	      if (frame) {
   633	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   634	      }
   635	
   636	      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   637	        const platformHeading = stack.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
   638	          ".case-card-title-platform-heading-platform",
   639	        );
   640	        const deviceHeading = stack.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
   641	          ".case-card-title-platform-heading-factor",
   642	        );
   643	        const verticalDividers = Array.from(
   644	          stack.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
   645	            ".case-card-title-platform-divider",
   646	          ),
   647	        );
   648	
   649	        if (
   650	          !platformHeading ||
   651	          !deviceHeading ||
   652	          verticalDividers.length === 0
   653	        ) {
   654	          stack.removeAttribute("data-platform-row-divider-ready");
   655	          return;
   656	        }
   657	
   658	        const stackRect = stack.getBoundingClientRect();
   659	        const platformRect = platformHeading.getBoundingClientRect();
   660	        const deviceRect = deviceHeading.getBoundingClientRect();
   661	        const dividerRects = verticalDividers.map((divider) =>
   662	          divider.getBoundingClientRect(),
   663	        );
   664	        const leftmostDividerCenter = Math.min(
   665	          ...dividerRects.map((rect) => rect.left + rect.width / 2),
   666	        );
   667	        const rightmostDividerCenter = Math.max(
   668	          ...dividerRects.map((rect) => rect.left + rect.width / 2),
   669	        );
   670	        const dividerStartCenter = deviceRect.left + deviceRect.width / 2;
   671	        const dividerOverhang = Math.max(
   672	          0,
   673	          leftmostDividerCenter - dividerStartCenter,
   674	        );
   675	        const dividerEndCenter = rightmostDividerCenter + dividerOverhang;
   676	        const dividerStart = dividerStartCenter - stackRect.left;
   677	        const dividerEnd = dividerEndCenter - stackRect.left;
   678	        const dividerTop =
   679	          (platformRect.top +
   680	            platformRect.height / 2 +
   681	            deviceRect.top +
   682	            deviceRect.height / 2) /
   683	            2 -
   684	          stackRect.top;
   685	        const dividerWidth = Math.max(0, dividerEnd - dividerStart);
   686	
   687	        rowDivider.style.setProperty(
   688	          "--case-platform-row-divider-left",
   689	          `${dividerStart.toFixed(2)}px`,
   690	        );
   691	        rowDivider.style.setProperty(
   692	          "--case-platform-row-divider-top",
   693	          `${dividerTop.toFixed(2)}px`,
   694	        );
   695	        rowDivider.style.setProperty(
   696	          "--case-platform-row-divider-width",
   697	          `${dividerWidth.toFixed(2)}px`,
   698	        );
   699	        stack.toggleAttribute(
   700	          "data-platform-row-divider-ready",
   701	          dividerWidth > 0,
   702	        );
   703	      });
   704	    };
   705	
   706	    updateRowDivider();
   707	    document.fonts?.ready.then(updateRowDivider);
   708	
   709	    const resizeObserver =
   710	      typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined"
   711	        ? null
   712	        : new ResizeObserver(updateRowDivider);
   713	
   714	    resizeObserver?.observe(stack);
   715	    window.addEventListener("resize", updateRowDivider);
   716	
   717	    return () => {
   718	      if (frame) {
   719	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   720	      }
   721	
   722	      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
   723	      window.removeEventListener("resize", updateRowDivider);
   724	    };
   725	  }, [
   726	    deviceLabelKey,
   727	    hasTablet,
   728	    hasWeb,
   729	    mobilePlatformKey,
   730	    platformLabelKey,
   731	    showPlatformLabels,
   732	    stackGap,
   733	  ]);
   734	
   735	  if (stackGap) {
   736	    stackStyle["--project-device-stack-gap"] = stackGap;
   737	  }
   738	
   739	  if (showPlatformLabels) {
   740	    const platforms: Array<MobilePlatform | "web"> = [];
   741	    const factorDevices: Array<"phone" | "tablet"> = [];
   742	
   743	    if (mobilePlatforms.includes("android") || hasTablet) {
   744	      platforms.push("android");
   745	    }
   746	
   747	    if (mobilePlatforms.includes("apple")) {
   748	      platforms.push("apple");
   749	    }
   750	
   751	    if (hasWeb) {
   752	      platforms.push("web");
   753	    }
   754	
   755	    if (mobilePlatforms.length > 0) {
   756	      factorDevices.push("phone");
   757	    }
   758	
   759	    if (hasTablet) {
   760	      factorDevices.push("tablet");
   761	    }
   762	
   763	    const platformLabels =
   764	      customPlatformLabels ??
   765	      platforms.map((platform) =>
   766	        platform === "web" ? "WEB" : platform === "android" ? "AND" : "IOS",
   767	      );
   768	    const factorDeviceLabels =
   769	      deviceLabels ??
   770	      factorDevices.map((device) =>
   771	        device === "phone" ? "PHONE" : "TABLET",
   772	      );
   773	    const labelColumnCount = Math.max(
   774	      platformLabels.length,
   775	      factorDeviceLabels.length,
   776	      1,
   777	    );
   778	    const dividerGridRow = factorDeviceLabels.length > 0 ? "1 / 3" : 1;
   779	    const spanningItemDividerCount =
   780	      factorDeviceLabels.length > platformLabels.length
   781	        ? platformLabels.length
   782	        : Math.max(0, platformLabels.length - 1);
   783	    const platformListStyle = {
   784	      ...stackStyle,
   785	      gridTemplateColumns: getPlatformListGridTemplate(labelColumnCount),
   786	    };
   787	
   788	    return (
   789	      <span
   790	        className="carousel-button-project case-card-title-platform-list"
   791	        ref={stackRef}
   792	        style={platformListStyle}
   793	      >
   794	        <span className="case-card-title-platform-heading case-card-title-platform-heading-platform">
   795	          PLATFORM
   796	        </span>
   797	        <PlatformLabelDivider
   798	          gridColumn={2}
   799	          gridRow={dividerGridRow}
   800	          kind="grid"
   801	        />
   802	        <PlatformLabelSlots labels={platformLabels} row={1} />
   803	        <PlatformColumnDividers
   804	          columnCount={spanningItemDividerCount + 1}
   805	          gridRow={dividerGridRow}
   806	        />
   807	        {factorDeviceLabels.length > 0 ? (
   808	          <>
   809	            <span className="case-card-title-platform-heading case-card-title-platform-heading-factor">
   810	              DEVICE
   811	            </span>
   812	            <PlatformLabelSlots labels={factorDeviceLabels} row={2} />
   813	            <PlatformColumnDividers
   814	              columnCount={factorDeviceLabels.length}
   815	              gridRow={2}
   816	              partial
   817	              startIndex={spanningItemDividerCount}
   818	            />
   819	            <span
   820	              aria-hidden="true"
   821	              className="case-card-title-platform-row-divider"
   822	            />
   823	          </>
   824	        ) : null}
   825	      </span>
   826	    );
   827	  }
   828	
   829	  return (
   830	    <span className="carousel-button-project" ref={stackRef} style={stackStyle}>
   831	      {mobilePlatforms.map((platform) => (
   832	        <PhoneMark key={platform} mobilePlatforms={[platform]} />
   833	      ))}
   834	      {hasTablet ? <TabletMark /> : null}
   835	      {hasWeb ? <WebMark scale={webIconScale} /> : null}
   836	    </span>
   837	  );
   838	}
   839	
   840	function CarouselArrowMark() {
   841	  return (
   842	    <img
   843	      alt=""
   844	      aria-hidden
   845	      className="carousel-button-arrow-mark"
   846	      draggable={false}
   847	      style={{
   848	        display: "block",
   849	        height: "100%",
   850	        verticalAlign: "top",
   851	        width: "100%",
   852	      }}
   853	      src="/images/carousel-arrow.svg"
   854	    />
   855	  );
   856	}
   857	
   858	export function ProjectCarouselButton() {
   859	  const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(0);
   860	  const projectButtonRef = useRef<HTMLAnchorElement>(null);
   861	  const activeProject = projectLinks[activeIndex];
   862	
   863	  const handleArrowClick = (event: MouseEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>) => {
   864	    event.preventDefault();
   865	    scrollCaseStudyRowIntoView(activeProject.caseStudyId);
   866	  };
   867	
   868	  useEffect(() => {
   869	    const interval = window.setInterval(() => {
   870	      setActiveIndex((currentIndex) => (currentIndex + 1) % projectLinks.length);
   871	    }, 5000);
   872	
   873	    return () => window.clearInterval(interval);
   874	  }, []);
   875	
   876	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   877	    let frame = 0;
   878	
   879	    const syncHeroActionGeometry = () => {
   880	      if (frame) {
   881	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   882	      }
   883	
   884	      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   885	        const heroCopy = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-copy");
   886	        const heroActions =
   887	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-actions");
   888	        const proofPanel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".proof-panel");
   889	        const stackRow = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".stack-row");
   890	        const heroVisual = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-visual");
   891	        const phoneStage = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".phone-stage");
   892	        const navBar = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".nav-bar");
   893	        const profilePhoto =
   894	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".profile-photo");
   895	        const socialButton =
   896	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-social-button");
   897	        const visualFrameSelectors = [
   898	          ".profile-photo",
   899	          ".phone-shell-commerce",
   900	          ".phone-shell-finance",
   901	          ".tablet-shell",
   902	        ];
   903	
   904	        if (proofPanel && profilePhoto) {
   905	          const proofRect = proofPanel.getBoundingClientRect();
   906	          const profileRect = profilePhoto.getBoundingClientRect();
   907	          const proofWidth = profileRect.right - proofRect.left;
   908	
   909	          if (proofWidth > 0) {
   910	            [heroCopy, proofPanel, stackRow].forEach((element) => {
   911	              element?.style.setProperty(
   912	                "--hero-proof-landscape-width",
   913	                `${proofWidth}px`,
   914	              );
   915	            });
   916	          }
   917	        }
   918	
   919	        if (heroVisual && navBar && stackRow) {
   920	          const isTwoColumnHero = window.matchMedia(
   921	            "(min-width: 1041px), (min-width: 981px) and (orientation: landscape)",
   922	          ).matches;
   923	
   924	          if (!isTwoColumnHero) {
   925	            heroVisual.style.removeProperty("--hero-visual-center-offset");
   926	          } else {
   927	            const isCompactLandscapeHero = window.matchMedia(
   928	              "(min-width: 981px) and (max-width: 1023px) and (orientation: landscape)",
   929	            ).matches;
   930	            const heroVisualScale =
   931	              heroVisual.offsetWidth > 0
   932	                ? heroVisual.getBoundingClientRect().width / heroVisual.offsetWidth
   933	                : 1;
   934	            const deviceStageSnap = isCompactLandscapeHero
   935	              ? (Number.parseFloat(
   936	                  window
   937	                    .getComputedStyle(phoneStage ?? heroVisual)
   938	                    .getPropertyValue("--hero-device-stage-snap"),
   939	                ) || 0) * heroVisualScale
   940	              : 0;
   941	            const visualRects = visualFrameSelectors
   942	              .map((selector) => ({
   943	                element: document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(selector),
   944	                selector,
   945	              }))
   946	              .filter(
   947	                (
   948	                  entry,
   949	                ): entry is { element: HTMLElement; selector: string } =>
   950	                  Boolean(entry.element),
   951	              )
   952	              .map(({ element, selector }) => {
   953	                const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
   954	                const snapAdjustment =
   955	                  selector === ".profile-photo" ? 0 : deviceStageSnap;
   956	
   957	                return {
   958	                  bottom: rect.bottom - snapAdjustment,
   959	                  top: rect.top - snapAdjustment,
   960	                };
   961	              });
   962	
   963	            if (visualRects.length > 0) {
   964	              const navRect = navBar.getBoundingClientRect();
   965	              const stackRect = stackRow.getBoundingClientRect();
   966	              const visualTop = Math.min(
   967	                ...visualRects.map((rect) => rect.top),
   968	              );
   969	              const visualBottom = Math.max(
   970	                ...visualRects.map((rect) => rect.bottom),
   971	              );
   972	              const currentOffset =
   973	                Number.parseFloat(
   974	                  window
   975	                    .getComputedStyle(heroVisual)
   976	                    .getPropertyValue("--hero-visual-center-offset"),
   977	                ) || 0;
   978	              const visualCenterWithoutOffset =
   979	                (visualTop + visualBottom) / 2 - currentOffset;
   980	              const targetCenter = (navRect.bottom + stackRect.top) / 2;
   981	              const nextOffset = targetCenter - visualCenterWithoutOffset;
   982	
   983	              heroVisual.style.setProperty(
   984	                "--hero-visual-center-offset",
   985	                `${nextOffset}px`,
   986	              );
   987	            }
   988	          }
   989	        }
   990	
   991	        if (heroActions && socialButton) {
   992	          const heroActionsWidth = heroActions.getBoundingClientRect().width;
   993	          const socialButtonHeight =
   994	            socialButton.getBoundingClientRect().height;
   995	
   996	          if (heroCopy && heroActionsWidth > 0) {
   997	            heroCopy.style.setProperty(
   998	              "--hero-text-stack-width",
   999	              `${heroActionsWidth}px`,
  1000	            );
  1001	          }
  1002	
  1003	          if (socialButtonHeight > 0) {
  1004	            const setPx = (name: string, value: number) => {
  1005	              heroActions.style.setProperty(name, `${value}px`);
  1006	            };
  1007	
  1008	            setPx("--hero-social-button-height", socialButtonHeight);
  1009	            setPx("--hero-social-font-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.295833);
  1010	            setPx("--hero-social-icon-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.672);
  1011	            const baseSocialButtonHeight = 48;
  1012	            const baseSocialGap = baseSocialButtonHeight * 0.045833 + 1;
  1013	            const stretchedSocialGap =
  1014	              Math.max(0, socialButtonHeight - baseSocialButtonHeight) *
  1015	              0.091666;
  1016	
  1017	            setPx("--hero-social-gap", baseSocialGap + stretchedSocialGap);
  1018	            setPx("--hero-social-pad-x", socialButtonHeight / 8);
  1019	            setPx("--hero-view-label-font-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.275);
  1020	            heroCopy?.style.setProperty(
  1021	              "--hero-eyebrow-font-size",
  1022	              `${socialButtonHeight * 0.367}px`,
  1023	            );
  1024	            heroCopy?.style.setProperty(
  1025	              "--hero-lede-font-size",
  1026	              `${socialButtonHeight * 0.5}px`,
  1027	            );
  1028	            setPx(
  1029	              "--hero-carousel-base-font-size",
  1030	              socialButtonHeight * 0.34375,
  1031	            );
  1032	            setPx(
  1033	              "--hero-carousel-name-font-size",
  1034	              socialButtonHeight * 0.352917,
  1035	            );
  1036	            setPx(
  1037	              "--hero-carousel-category-font-size",
  1038	              socialButtonHeight * 0.176458,
  1039	            );
  1040	            setPx(
  1041	              "--hero-carousel-arrow-font-size",
  1042	              socialButtonHeight * 0.458333,
  1043	            );
  1044	          }
  1045	        }
  1046	
  1047	        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("hero-stack-sync"));
  1048	
  1049	        const viewLabel =
  1050	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-view-label");
  1051	        const githubText =
  1052	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".github-label-text");
  1053	        const githubIcon =
  1054	          document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".github-icon-mark");
  1055	
  1056	        if (viewLabel && githubText && githubIcon) {
  1057	          const viewLabelRect = viewLabel.getBoundingClientRect();
  1058	          const githubTextRect = githubText.getBoundingClientRect();
  1059	          const githubIconRect = githubIcon.getBoundingClientRect();
  1060	          const clusterLeft = Math.min(githubTextRect.left, githubIconRect.left);
  1061	          const stackLeft = clusterLeft - viewLabelRect.left;
  1062	
  1063	          viewLabel.style.setProperty(
  1064	            "--hero-view-stack-left",
  1065	            `${stackLeft}px`,
  1066	          );
  1067	        }
  1068	      });
  1069	    };
  1070	
  1071	    syncHeroActionGeometry();
  1072	    window.addEventListener("resize", syncHeroActionGeometry);
  1073	
  1074	    const targetIcon =
  1075	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".dribbble-icon-mark") ??
  1076	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".linkedin-icon-mark");
  1077	    const navBar = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".nav-bar");
  1078	    const heroVisual = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-visual");
  1079	    const heroActions = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-actions");
  1080	    const stackRow = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".stack-row");
  1081	    const proofPanel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".proof-panel");
  1082	    const profilePhoto = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".profile-photo");
  1083	    const targetButton = targetIcon?.closest(".hero-social-button");
  1084	    const viewLabel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-view-label");
  1085	    const githubText = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".github-label-text");
  1086	    const githubIcon =
  1087	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".github-icon-mark");
  1088	    const githubButton = githubText?.closest(".github-button");
  1089	    const resizeObserver =
  1090	      typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined"
  1091	        ? null
  1092	        : new ResizeObserver(syncHeroActionGeometry);
  1093	
  1094	    if (resizeObserver) {
  1095	      if (projectButtonRef.current) {
  1096	        resizeObserver.observe(projectButtonRef.current);
  1097	      }
  1098	
  1099	      if (heroActions) {
  1100	        resizeObserver.observe(heroActions);
  1101	      }
  1102	
  1103	      if (navBar) {
  1104	        resizeObserver.observe(navBar);
  1105	      }
  1106	
  1107	      if (heroVisual) {
  1108	        resizeObserver.observe(heroVisual);
  1109	      }
  1110	
  1111	      if (stackRow) {
  1112	        resizeObserver.observe(stackRow);
  1113	      }
  1114	
  1115	      if (proofPanel) {
  1116	        resizeObserver.observe(proofPanel);
  1117	      }
  1118	
  1119	      if (profilePhoto) {
  1120	        resizeObserver.observe(profilePhoto);
  1121	      }
  1122	
  1123	      if (targetIcon) {
  1124	        resizeObserver.observe(targetIcon);
  1125	      }
  1126	
  1127	      if (targetButton) {
  1128	        resizeObserver.observe(targetButton);
  1129	      }
  1130	
  1131	      if (viewLabel) {
  1132	        resizeObserver.observe(viewLabel);
  1133	      }
  1134	
  1135	      if (githubText) {
  1136	        resizeObserver.observe(githubText);
  1137	      }
  1138	
  1139	      if (githubIcon) {
  1140	        resizeObserver.observe(githubIcon);
  1141	      }
  1142	
  1143	      if (githubButton) {
  1144	        resizeObserver.observe(githubButton);
  1145	      }
  1146	    }
  1147	
  1148	    if ("fonts" in document) {
  1149	      document.fonts.ready.then(syncHeroActionGeometry).catch(() => {});
  1150	    }
  1151	
  1152	    return () => {
  1153	      if (frame) {
  1154	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  1155	      }
  1156	
  1157	      window.removeEventListener("resize", syncHeroActionGeometry);
  1158	      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
  1159	    };
  1160	  }, []);
  1161	
  1162	  return (
  1163	    <span className="carousel-button-group">
  1164	      <a
  1165	        aria-label={`Open ${activeProject.name} project`}
  1166	        className="button button-primary carousel-button"
  1167	        href={activeProject.href}
  1168	        ref={projectButtonRef}
  1169	        rel="noreferrer"
  1170	        target="_blank"
  1171	      >
  1172	        <span className="carousel-button-window" aria-live="polite">
  1173	          <span className="carousel-button-label" key={activeProject.href}>
  1174	            <span
  1175	              className={`carousel-button-action carousel-button-action-${activeProject.tone}`}
  1176	              style={{
  1177	                ...strokedWhiteTextStyle,
  1178	                alignItems: "center",
  1179	                color: projectNameColors[activeProject.tone],
  1180	                display: "inline-flex",
  1181	                flexDirection: "column",
  1182	                fontSize: "var(--hero-carousel-name-font-size)",
  1183	                gap: "2.2px",
  1184	                justifyContent: "center",
  1185	                lineHeight: 0.95,
  1186	              }}
  1187	            >
  1188	              <span
  1189	                className="carousel-button-action-name"
  1190	                style={{ fontSize: activeProject.nameFontSize }}
  1191	              >
  1192	                {activeProject.name}
  1193	              </span>
  1194	              <span
  1195	                className="carousel-button-action-category"
  1196	                style={{
  1197	                  WebkitTextStroke: 0,
  1198	                  color: "#000",
  1199	                  fontSize: "var(--hero-carousel-category-font-size)",
  1200	                  fontWeight: 900,
  1201	                  letterSpacing: "0.06em",
  1202	                  lineHeight: 1,
  1203	                  textAlign: "center",
  1204	                  textShadow: "none",
  1205	                }}
  1206	              >
  1207	                {activeProject.category}
  1208	              </span>
  1209	            </span>
  1210	            <ProjectDeviceStack
  1211	              color={projectNameColors[activeProject.tone]}
  1212	              hasTablet={activeProject.hasTablet}
  1213	              hasWeb={activeProject.hasWeb}
  1214	              mobilePlatforms={activeProject.mobilePlatforms}
  1215	            />
  1216	          </span>
  1217	        </span>
  1218	      </a>
  1219	      <a
  1220	        aria-label={`Scroll to ${activeProject.name} case study`}
  1221	        className="button button-primary carousel-arrow-button"
  1222	        href={`#${activeProject.caseStudyId}`}
  1223	        onClick={handleArrowClick}
  1224	      >
  1225	        <span aria-hidden className="carousel-button-arrow">
  1226	          <CarouselArrowMark />
  1227	        </span>
  1228	      </a>
  1229	    </span>
  1230	  );
  1231	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Prahl.dev Portfolio Site
     2	
     3	Next.js + Tailwind CSS portfolio start page for Prahl.dev.
     4	
     5	## What is included
     6	
     7	- `app/page.tsx` - the homepage/start page.
     8	- `app/globals.css` - the green/black visual system and responsive layout.
     9	- `public/images/profile-placeholder.png` - generated AI profile placeholder; replace this with your own square photo later using the same filename.
    10	- `public/images/start-page-mockup-reference.png` - the original generated mockup reference.
    11	- `public/favicon.svg` - simple green/black monogram favicon.
    12	
    13	## Local development
    14	
    15	```sh
    16	npm install
    17	npm run dev
    18	```
    19	
    20	Open `http://localhost:3000`.
    21	
    22	## Production build
    23	
    24	For Vercel, Netlify, or another Next.js host:
    25	
    26	```sh
    27	npm run build
    28	```
    29	
    30	For a simple static host:
    31	
    32	```sh
    33	npm run build:static
    34	```
    35	
    36	Then upload the generated `out/` folder.
    37	
    38	## Replace the profile photo
    39	
    40	Put your real square profile photo at:
    41	
    42	```txt
    43	public/images/profile-placeholder.png
    44	```
    45	
    46	Keep the filename the same and rebuild.


============================================================
FILE: DEPLOYMENT.md
============================================================

     1	# Deployment Notes
     2	
     3	## Best Option: Vercel
     4	
     5	1. Upload this `portfolio-site` folder to a GitHub repo.
     6	2. Import the repo in Vercel.
     7	3. Use the default Next.js settings.
     8	4. Build command: `npm run build`.
     9	
    10	## Netlify
    11	
    12	1. Upload this folder to a GitHub repo.
    13	2. Connect the repo in Netlify.
    14	3. Build command: `npm run build`.
    15	4. Publish directory: `.next`.
    16	5. Enable Netlify's Next.js runtime if prompted.
    17	
    18	## Simple Static Hosting
    19	
    20	Use this when the host only accepts HTML/CSS/JS files:
    21	
    22	```sh
    23	npm install
    24	npm run build:static
    25	```
    26	
    27	Upload the contents of:
    28	
    29	```txt
    30	out/
    31	```
    32	
    33	This mode does not need a running Node.js server after upload.
    34	
    35	## Contact Button
    36	
    37	The contact button currently uses:
    38	
    39	```txt
    40	mailto:hello@example.com
    41	```
    42	
    43	Replace `hello@example.com` in `app/page.tsx` with your real email address.

FILE: .github/workflows/hostinger-static-export.yml
============================================================

     1	name: Build static export for Hostinger
     2	
     3	on:
     4	  push:
     5	    branches: [main]
     6	  workflow_dispatch:
     7	
     8	permissions:
     9	  contents: write
    10	
    11	concurrency:
    12	  group: hostinger-static-export
    13	  cancel-in-progress: true
    14	
    15	jobs:
    16	  build-and-publish:
    17	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    18	    steps:
    19	      - name: Check out source
    20	        uses: actions/checkout@v4
    21	
    22	      - name: Set up Node
    23	        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
    24	        with:
    25	          node-version: 22
    26	          cache: npm
    27	
    28	      - name: Install dependencies
    29	        run: npm ci
    30	
    31	      - name: Build static export
    32	        run: npm run build:static
    33	
    34	      - name: Publish static export branch
    35	        env:
    36	          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
    37	        run: |
    38	          cd out
    39	          git init -b hostinger-deploy
    40	          git config user.name "github-actions[bot]"
    41	          git config user.email "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"
    42	          git add -A
    43	          git commit -m "Deploy static export from ${GITHUB_SHA}"
    44	          git remote add origin "https://x-access-token:${GITHUB_TOKEN}@github.com/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}.git"
    45	          git push --force origin hostinger-deploy

FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/portfolio_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Portfolio project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Portfolio Next.js PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you do not understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	10. For this Portfolio repo/website project, always ask me for the commit message before committing or pushing.
    28	
    29	
    30	============================================================
    31	PORTFOLIO PROJECT NOTES
    32	============================================================
    33	
    34	- Main source branch: main
    35	- Deployment source: GitHub Actions builds main into the hostinger-deploy branch
    36	- Hostinger root directory: public_html
    37	- Main stack represented by the website: React / React Native, Next.js, Tailwind CSS, Node.js, Kotlin
    38	- Public website assets live under public/
    39	- Run this script before starting a Portfolio session to refresh the project snapshot.
    40	
    41	
    42	============================================================
    43	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    44	============================================================
    45	
    46	This file is rebuilt from the current Portfolio workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    47	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    48	
    49	The snapshot intentionally ignores generated/cache/runtime/private files:
    50	- .git
    51	- node_modules
    52	- .next
    53	- .next.*
    54	- out
    55	- dist
    56	- build
    57	- .cache
    58	- .turbo
    59	- .npm
    60	- .pnpm-store
    61	- .yarn
    62	- .DS_Store
    63	- next-env.d.ts
    64	- *.tsbuildinfo
    65	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    66	- *.zip
    67	- .env and .env*.local
    68	
    69	Binary image, PDF, and archive files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    70	
    71	
    72	============================================================
    73	PROJECT FILE TREE
    74	============================================================
    75	
    76	EOF
    77	}
    78	
    79	print_file() {
    80	  local file="$1"
    81	
    82	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    83	    {
    84	      echo ""
    85	      echo ""
    86	      echo "============================================================"
    87	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    88	      echo "============================================================"
    89	      echo ""
    90	      nl -ba "$file"
    91	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    92	  fi
    93	}
    94	
    95	append_project_tree() {
    96	  # Use Git's own ignore rules so the snapshot cannot drift from .gitignore.
    97	  # Include tracked files plus untracked files that Git does not ignore.
    98	  printf '%s\n' "$PROJECT_DIR" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    99	
   100	  git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" ls-files --cached --others --exclude-standard \
   101	    | sort \
   102	    | while IFS= read -r relative_file; do
   103	      case "$relative_file" in
   104	        .git/*|node_modules/*|.next/*|.next.*/*|out/*|dist/*|build/*|.cache/*|.turbo/*|.npm/*|.pnpm-store/*|.yarn/*|.DS_Store|*/.DS_Store|*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt|*.zip|.env|.env*.local|next-env.d.ts|*.tsbuildinfo)
   105	          continue
   106	          ;;
   107	      esac
   108	
   109	      printf '%s/%s\n' "$PROJECT_DIR" "$relative_file" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   110	    done
   111	}
   112	
   113	print_inventory_header() {
   114	  local title="$1"
   115	
   116	  {
   117	    echo ""
   118	    echo ""
   119	    echo "============================================================"
   120	    echo "$title"
   121	    echo "============================================================"
   122	    echo ""
   123	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   124	}
   125	
   126	print_file_inventory_line() {
   127	  local file="$1"
   128	  local size
   129	  local modified
   130	
   131	  size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   132	  modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   133	  printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   134	}
   135	
   136	print_dir_inventory() {
   137	  local title="$1"
   138	  local dir="$2"
   139	
   140	  print_inventory_header "$title"
   141	
   142	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
   143	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   144	    return
   145	  fi
   146	
   147	  find "$dir" -type f \
   148	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
   149	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
   150	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   151	    print_file_inventory_line "$file"
   152	  done
   153	}
   154	
   155	print_matching_inventory() {
   156	  local title="$1"
   157	  shift
   158	
   159	  print_inventory_header "$title"
   160	
   161	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" "$@" -type f \
   162	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git/*" \
   163	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/node_modules/*" \
   164	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.next/*" \
   165	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.next.*/*" \
   166	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/out/*" \
   167	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
   168	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
   169	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   170	    print_file_inventory_line "$file"
   171	  done
   172	}
   173	
   174	append_git_context() {
   175	  {
   176	    echo ""
   177	    echo ""
   178	    echo "============================================================"
   179	    echo "GIT CONTEXT"
   180	    echo "============================================================"
   181	    echo ""
   182	    echo "Current branch:"
   183	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" branch --show-current 2>/dev/null || true
   184	    echo ""
   185	    echo "Status:"
   186	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" status --short --branch 2>/dev/null || true
   187	    echo ""
   188	    echo "Recent commits:"
   189	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" log --oneline --decorate -10 2>/dev/null || true
   190	    echo ""
   191	    echo "Remotes:"
   192	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" remote -v 2>/dev/null || true
   193	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   194	}
   195	
   196	write_snapshot() {
   197	  write_header
   198	
   199	  append_project_tree
   200	
   201	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   202	
   203	
   204	============================================================
   205	FILE CONTENTS - APP SOURCE
   206	============================================================
   207	
   208	EOF
   209	
   210	  for file in \
   211	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/layout.tsx" \
   212	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/page.tsx" \
   213	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/ProjectCarouselButton.tsx" \
   214	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/globals.css"
   215	  do
   216	    print_file "$file"
   217	  done
   218	
   219	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   220	
   221	
   222	============================================================
   223	FILE CONTENTS - ROOT / TOOLING / DEPLOYMENT
   224	============================================================
   225	
   226	EOF
   227	
   228	  for file in \
   229	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.gitignore" \
   230	    "$PROJECT_DIR/README.md" \
   231	    "$PROJECT_DIR/DEPLOYMENT.md" \
   232	    "$PROJECT_DIR/package.json" \
   233	    "$PROJECT_DIR/package-lock.json" \
   234	    "$PROJECT_DIR/next.config.ts" \
   235	    "$PROJECT_DIR/postcss.config.mjs" \
   236	    "$PROJECT_DIR/tsconfig.json" \
   237	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.github/workflows/hostinger-static-export.yml" \
   238	    "$PROJECT_DIR/public/favicon.svg" \
   239	    "$PROJECT_DIR/read_project.sh"
   240	  do
   241	    print_file "$file"
   242	  done
   243	
   244	  append_git_context
   245	
   246	  print_dir_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY - PUBLIC IMAGES" "$PROJECT_DIR/public/images"
   247	  print_matching_inventory "PDF WORKLOG INVENTORY" \( -name "portfolio_worklog_*.pdf" \)
   248	  print_matching_inventory "DEPLOYMENT ARCHIVE INVENTORY" \( -name "*.zip" \)
   249	
   250	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   251	
   252	
   253	============================================================
   254	NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
   255	============================================================
   256	
   257	The Portfolio snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private paths:
   258	- .git
   259	- node_modules
   260	- .next
   261	- .next.*
   262	- out
   263	- dist
   264	- build
   265	- .cache
   266	- .turbo
   267	- .npm
   268	- .pnpm-store
   269	- .yarn
   270	- .DS_Store
   271	- next-env.d.ts
   272	- *.tsbuildinfo
   273	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
   274	- *.zip
   275	- .env and .env*.local
   276	
   277	The snapshot lists public images, PDF worklogs, and deployment archives by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF/ZIP bytes.
   278	
   279	EOF
   280	}
   281	
   282	write_snapshot
   283	
   284	echo "Done."
   285	echo ""
   286	echo "Snapshot created here:"
   287	echo "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   288	echo ""
   289	echo "Open it with:"
   290	echo "open \"$OUTPUT_FILE\""

============================================================
NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
============================================================

The Portfolio snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private paths:
- .git
- node_modules
- .next
- .next.*
- out
- dist
- build
- .cache
- .turbo
- .npm
- .pnpm-store
- .yarn
- .DS_Store
- next-env.d.ts
- *.tsbuildinfo
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.zip
- .env and .env*.local

The snapshot lists public images, PDF worklogs, and deployment archives by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF/ZIP bytes.
